math.tan(math.pi * cutoff / fs)\n    poles = []\n    for k in range(order):\n        angle = math.pi * (2 * k + 1 + order) / (2 * order)\n        sk = complex(w * math.cos(angle), w * math.sin(angle))\n        zk = (2.0 + sk) / (2.0 - sk)\n        poles.append(zk)\n\n    a = [1.0]\n    for p in poles:\n        new_a = [0.0] * (len(a) + 1)\n        for i, val in enumerate(a):\n            new_a[i] += val\n            new_a[i + 1] -= val * p\n        a = new_a\n\n    a_real = [val.real for val in a]\n    b_binom = [math.comb(order, k) for k in range(order + 1)]\n    sum_a = back_pad = [2 * x[-1] - v for v in x[-2:-pad_len - 2:-1]]\n    padded = front_pad + list(x) + back_pad\n    y_fwd = _fallback_lfilter(b, a, padded)\n    y_rev = _fallback_lfilter(b, a, y_fwd[::-1])\n def set_pixel(x, y, r, g, b):\n        if 0 <= x < width and 0 <= y < height:\n            idx = (y * width + x) * 3\n            pixels[idx] = r\n            pixels[idx + 1] = g\n err = dx - dy\n        while True:\n            set_pixel(x0, y0, r, g, b)\n            if x0 == x1 and y0 == y1:\n                break\n            e2 = 2 * err\n            if e2 > -dy:\n                err -= dy\n                x0 += sx\n            if e2 < dx:\n                err += dx\n                y0 += sy\n\n    draw_fn(set_pixel, draw_line, width, height)\n\n    raw_data = --input data/input_signal.csv --output output/filtered_signal.csv --plot-dir output\n```\n\n## Outputs\n\nThe script generates the following artifacts:\n\n- `output/filtered_signal.csv`: Processed signal containing `time` and `filtered_amplitude` columns with original time values preserved.\n- `output/raw_signal.png`: Plot of the raw sensor signal across time.\n- `output/filtered_signal.png` printed to stdout, including duration, mean, minimum, maximum, and dominant frequency.\n\n## Reusable Functions\n\n`signal_analysis.py` exposes modular functions for custom workflows:\n\n- `load_signal(file_path)`: Loads and validates CSV data.\n- `estimate_sampling_frequency(time)`: Computes sampling rate from time intervals.\n- `butter_lowpass(cutoff, fs, order)`: Generates Butterworth filter coefficients.\n- `butter_lowpass_filter(data, cutoff, fs, order)`: Applies Butterworth filter to data.\n- `compute_fft(data, fs)`: Computes frequency bins and magnitude spectrum.\n- `compute_dominant_frequency(data, fs)`: Identifies dominant non-DC frequency component.\n- `compute_summary_statistics(time, amplitude, fs)`: Calculates duration, mean, min, max, and dominant
